| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R085` | **Medulla approves immutable source queries for bounded connector execution (rollup).** Subsystem: sources. Medulla owns read-only query meaning and authorization. The SDK client executes an approved query through its registered adapter (`SCION-SOURCE-INGEST-R004.7` to `R004.12`). Entry points: new methods `ApproveSourceQuery` and `VerifySourceQueryApproval`. Existing R015, R043, R069 and R085.1 stay unchanged. Children `.2` to `.8` deliver the producer and its served evidence. | Root L3 journey test: `tests/source_query_approval_served.rs::approval_to_verification_journey` (L3). The cross-repository journey is `SCION-SOURCE-INGEST-R004.12` (L5). |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R085.1` | **Model.** Add `SourceQueryRequest`, `ApprovedSourceQuery`, `SourceQueryApprovalCheck`, `SourceQueryApprovalValidity` and `SourceQueryLimits` in planned `crates/medulla-types/src/source_query.rs`. The exact fields and methods are specified in the plan. Bind typed parameters, dialect, source/schema/snapshot identities, read-only operations, authority, expiry and finite limits. Reject unknown fields and unrepresentable parameter values. Proving test: `crates/medulla-types/src/source_query.rs::tests::approval_records_round_trip` (planned). | Round trips preserve every binding. Invalid references, parameter types, nonfinite values, absent snapshot identity and zero/unbounded limits refuse. |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R085.2` | **Behavior: issue approval.** Subsystem: sources. Caller chain: wire dispatch → `route_process_global_domains` in `src/server/dispatch/graph_pipeline/pipeline.rs` → `try_handle` in `src/server/handlers/federation.rs`. `ApproveSourceQuery` validates the registered source and query under the verified carrier, then issues `ApprovedSourceQuery`. It reuses the control-lease issue path `apply_issue_control_lease_row` in `src/redb_store/work_item/control_lease.rs`. Add the reserved kind constant `SOURCE_QUERY_APPROVAL_KIND = "source.query.approval"` in `crates/medulla-types/src/control_lease.rs`. `IssueControlLeaseRequest::validate` refuses that kind with `ACCESS_DENIED`, as it does for `rbac.elevation`. The grant digest (SHA-256) binds every approved field. The server sets issue time and clamps expiry to `knob source_query.approval_ttl_s` (300, [10, 3600], seconds). Depends on: R085.1, MEDULLA-DURABLE-KERNEL-R075. | Test: `src/server/handlers/federation/source_query_tests.rs::approval_requires_authority_and_native_commit` (L3). A generic `IssueControlLease` of the reserved kind must refuse. External SQL never runs during approval. |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R085.3` | **Behavior: verify approval.** Subsystem: sources. Caller chain: wire dispatch → `route_process_global_domains` in `src/server/dispatch/graph_pipeline/pipeline.rs` → `try_handle` in `src/server/handlers/federation.rs`. `VerifySourceQueryApproval` takes the complete descriptor and the observed source, schema and snapshot identities. It recomputes the digest and reads the lease with `read_control_lease` in `control_lease.rs`. It requires active status, server time before both expiries, matching caller and delegation, and current source permissions. It rechecks policy, identity and source revisions. A supplied lease view is not proof. It grants no SQL effect and mints no bearer credential. Refusals: `approval_expired`, `approval_revoked`, `stale_source`, `unauthorized`, `verification_unavailable`. Depends on: R085.2. | Test: `src/server/handlers/federation/source_query_tests.rs::changed_or_revoked_approval_refuses` (L3). Changing any binding, or a revoked, expired, consumed or missing lease, refuses. |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R085.4` | **Behavior: read-only SQL.** Subsystem: sources. Caller: `ApproveSourceQuery` → `validate_federated_sql` in `crates/medulla-plan/src/federation.rs` → the R069 rendering helpers `render_sql_columns` in `crates/medulla-plan/src/federation_opt/columns.rs`. Medulla refuses by classification: DDL, DML, COPY, external-table creation, multiple statements, locking reads and unknown functions or dialects. Typed parameters stay separate from SQL text. The descriptor requires `read_only_transaction: true`. The SDK must open a read-only database transaction (`SCION-SOURCE-INGEST-R004.8`). Medulla cannot enforce that inside the driver. Refusals: `unsupported_dialect`, `invalid_query`. Depends on: R085.1. | Test: `crates/medulla-plan/src/federation_tests.rs::approved_source_query_has_no_write_effects` (L1) and the root slice test. Unknown dialects, functions or effects refuse without issuing approval. |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R085.5` | **Wiring.** Subsystem: sources. Caller chain: wire dispatch → `route_process_global_domains` in `src/server/dispatch/graph_pipeline/pipeline.rs` → `try_handle` in `src/server/handlers/federation.rs`. Register both methods in `crates/medulla-capabilities/src/domains/query.rs` with authorization and durability metadata. Issue is durable through the control-lease commit route in `src/server/dispatch/graph_pipeline/native_routes.rs`. Verify is an authoritative read. Add the `Method` variants in `crates/medulla-types/src/protocol/method/method_05.rs`. The Rust serde types in `source_query.rs` are the contract, and the SDK client hand-mirrors them (`SCION-SOURCE-INGEST-R004.7`). Arbora serves both as `eg.*` operations with no new row. Depends on: R085.2, R085.3. | Test: `tests/source_query_approval_served.rs::real_dispatch_issues_and_verifies_approval` (L3) asserts no parallel planner, store or client-side approval authority. |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R085.6` | **Behavior: bounded execution contract.** Subsystem: sources. Limits live in `SourceQueryLimits` of R085.1. Medulla clamps each caller value to the tenant ceiling and rejects zero. Ceilings (all positive): `knob source_query.max_rows` 1000000 [1, 100000000] rows. `knob source_query.max_bytes` 268435456 [1024, 17179869184] bytes. `knob source_query.chunk_rows` 5000 [1, 100000] rows. `knob source_query.chunk_bytes` 4194304 [1024, 67108864] bytes. `knob source_query.queue_depth` 4 [1, 64] chunks. `knob source_query.queue_bytes` 16777216 [1024, 1073741824] bytes. `knob source_query.deadline_ms` 60000 [1000, 3600000] ms. `knob source_query.resident_bytes` 134217728 [1048576, 8589934592] bytes. The SDK enforces backpressure and keeps no full result (`SCION-SOURCE-INGEST-R004.8`). It verifies approval before opening the driver, before each chunk and before completion. Depends on: R085.3. | Test: `src/server/handlers/federation/source_query_tests.rs::limits_clamp_to_tenant_ceiling` (L3). The SDK side is proved by `SCION-SOURCE-INGEST-R004.12` (L5). |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R085.7` | **Refusal.** Subsystem: sources. Add the closed error set in new `crates/medulla-types/src/source_query.rs`: `unsupported_dialect`, `invalid_query`, `unauthorized`, `approval_expired`, `approval_revoked`, `stale_source`, `query_failed`, `limit_exceeded`, `cancelled`, `verification_unavailable`. Errors carry query identity, stage and last completed chunk. They never carry SQL literals, parameter values or credentials. `SCION-SOURCE-INGEST-R004.10` keeps these reasons after partial output. Depends on: R085.1. | Test: `crates/medulla-types/src/source_query.rs::tests::errors_redact_literals_and_round_trip` (L1) and the root slice test. |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R085.8` | **Evidence and hot-path proof.** Subsystem: sources. Entry point: the two real methods. The medulla L3 journey issues an approval, verifies it, revokes it and verifies again, using a stub driver. It asserts immutable provenance, authorization refusal and zero database effects. It records the approval and verification receipts. The cross-repository journey with a real SQL database and memory measurement is `SCION-SOURCE-INGEST-R004.12` (L5). Memory tolerance lives in the SDK row. Depends on: R085.5, R085.7. | Test: `tests/source_query_approval_served.rs::approval_to_verification_journey` (L3) with two tenants and one cross-tenant negative case. |

<!-- spec-definition-patch:owner-batch-20261012-federated-query-and-reasoning -->
## Source-backed mapped requirement dispositions — owner batch 2026-10-12

| ID | Requirement | Verification |
|---|---|---|
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R006.1` | **Temporal rule-derived facts with persisted origin chain.** Stage: query execution plus graph write, served `Uql` with rules, persisted through `ApplyMutation`. Caller: served `Uql` → `dispatch_query_family` (`src/server/handlers/query/dispatch.rs:66`) → `handle_uql` (`src/server/handlers/query/uql_statement.rs:17`) → `crates/medulla-plan/src/rules/temporal.rs::derive` → mutation builder `derive_facts_mutation` → served `ApplyMutation` (`MEDULLA-DURABLE-KERNEL-R084`). Chains derive in event-time order. A malformed interval bound refuses `DERIVED_BOUND_MALFORMED`. Derived facts survive a restart. A served trace names each derivation step. Rule language: UQL rule form version `temporal-rules-v1`, intervals closed-open `[valid_from, valid_to)` over event time; a join needs a non-empty overlap. Derived-fact identity is the SHA-256 digest (64 lowercase hex characters) of rule digest, premise ids and interval. An update supersedes by revision; a retraction writes a tombstone revision. Origin record `DerivedFact{fact_id, rule_digest, premise_ids, origin_kind (sentence or axiom), origin_ref, graph_generation}` in `crates/medulla-types/src/derived_fact.rs`. Refusals: `RULE_VERSION_UNSUPPORTED`, `DERIVED_ORIGIN_MISSING`, `DERIVED_WINDOW_CONFLICT` (same identity, conflicting windows), `DERIVED_WRITE_UNAUTHORIZED`, `DERIVED_BOUND_MALFORMED`. All refusals leave the graph revision unchanged. Query proof premises are not a fact producer. Depends on: R006, R011, MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R003, MEDULLA-DURABLE-KERNEL-R084. | Test: `tests/query/test_temporal_rules_served.py::test_derived_fact_origin_readback` (L4). Two source facts have sentence and axiom origins. It covers matching and nonmatching intervals. It reads back rule digest, generation and origin refs. Each refusal leaves the revision unchanged. `tests/forward_chain_served.rs::chain_covers_model_order_origin_refusal_restart_and_trace` (L3) proves the six obligations: model, time-ordered chaining, origin readback, malformed-bound refusal, restart durability and served trace. |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R040.5` | **Low-storage ANN selectivity path (rollup).** Stage: query execution and ingest, served `UnifiedQuery`. Root family: retrieval. Children `.1` to `.6` deliver the bounded low-storage ANN. Memory admission, relations and retention belong to `MEDULLA-DURABLE-KERNEL-R085` (served `ApplyMutation`, `MemorySnapshot` keyed by tenant, memory id and source digest). This row declares only the retrieval cost bound. Bound: rows visited at most `4 * result_rows + 64` at corpus sizes 1000, 10000 and 100000. Filtered scans (R040) stay separate coverage. This row claims no model-weight change and no forgetting property. Depends on: R040, MEDULLA-DURABLE-KERNEL-R085. | The root journey test is `tests/retrieval/test_low_storage_ann_served.py::test_selectivity_bound_and_readback` (L4). Nightly group `retrieval/selectivity` reports rows, bytes and visits. |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R083.7` | **RETIRED: duplicate of the arbitration behavior and model rows.** Support-aware arbitration and the new fields now live in `R083.1` and `R083.2`. | Retired; see `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R083.2`. |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R081.10` | **Clock basis and bounded second pass.** Subsystem: retrieval decisions, second-pass step. Caller: `Method::RetrievalRound` → `run_round` → `choose_clock` and `plan_second_pass` in new `crates/medulla-plan/src/retrieval_clock.rs`. R081.8 owns follow-up expansion. This row adds two things. First, three typed clock fields on evidence rows: `storage_time`, `event_time` and `narrative_time`, each with source and provenance. When values disagree the precedence is `event_time`, then `narrative_time`, then `storage_time`. The result reports the chosen clock basis and the policy revision `clock-precedence`. No other heuristic picks a clock. Second, a bounded second pass. Sufficiency is the fraction of requested slots filled with supported evidence. Below `knob retrieval.second_pass_threshold` (0.5, [0, 1], fraction), `plan_second_pass` builds at most `knob retrieval.second_pass_queries` (3, [1, 8], queries) from the unmet slots. The result lists them. The harness runs one second round. Exhausted query budget returns a partial result with `RETRIEVAL_BUDGET_EXCEEDED`. Narrative time comes from the transcript ingest of MEDULLA-MEDIA-VOCAB; this row reads those fields. Depends on: R081.11, R081.8.3. | Test: `tests/retrieval_round_served.rs::clock_basis_and_second_pass_are_bounded` (L3) serves a fixture where all three clocks disagree and one where a clock is absent. It asserts basis, provenance, no second pass at sufficiency 0.5 or above and exactly the bounded queries below it. |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R028.1` | **Typed `KnowledgeSet` row contract.** Stage: query execution, served `Uql` `WITH KNOWLEDGE`. Caller: served `Uql` → `dispatch_query_family` (`src/server/handlers/query/dispatch.rs:66`) → `handle_uql` (`src/server/handlers/query/uql_statement.rs:17`) → `annotate.rs` → `KnowledgeSet::from_rowset`. Code: `KnowledgeSet` and `KnowledgeRow` in `crates/medulla-plan/src/knowledge.rs`; carrier `UqlKnowledge` in `crates/medulla-types/src/wire_query_core.rs`. Each row carries `provenance_frame` and `policy_frame`, each with state `Resolved`, `Missing` or `Stale`, beside `row id`, `source_refs`, `evidence_refs`, `policy_labels`. `Resolved` means both frames came from one pinned graph snapshot. The set stays a superset of a plain row set; id, score and projection are unchanged. Refusals: `KNOWLEDGE_FRAME_MISSING` and `KNOWLEDGE_FRAME_STALE` return no partial result. Foreign-row pushdown (R045) is unchanged. Codes register in `crates/medulla-capabilities/src/error_routing.rs`. Writes: none. Depends on: R028, R045. | Test: `tests/query/test_knowledge_set_uql_served.py::test_frames_resolved_missing_stale` (L4). It compares a plain row and a framed row against the structured producer. It asserts one refusal per omitted frame or snapshot binding. |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R005.1` | **Typed consistency-violation matrix on the validation result.** Stage: query execution, served `Uql` `VALIDATE SHAPE`. Caller: served `Uql` → `dispatch_query_family` (`src/server/handlers/query/dispatch.rs:66`) → `handle_uql` (`src/server/handlers/query/uql_statement.rs:17`) → `validate_shape` (`crates/medulla-plan/src/exec/dispatch/shape.rs`) → `violations::collect`. Code: `crates/medulla-types/src/shape_report.rs` defines `ViolationKind` (`temporal_conflict`, `cardinality`, `self_loop`, `asymmetry`, `ontology_defect`) and `Violation{kind, focus, relation_ids, graph_revision, ontology_revision, expected, observed, evidence_refs}`. Required fields differ by kind. Temporal conflict carries two intervals. Cardinality carries declared min, max and observed count. Self-loop carries the edge id. Asymmetry carries the axiom id, subject, object and both edge ids. Ontology defect carries the axiom id and affected terms. Intervals are closed-open `[valid_from, valid_to)` and overlap when `a.from < b.to` and `b.from < a.to`, the same rule as R006.1. An absent reverse edge is no violation. The validator reports every independent violation and never retracts. Refusals: `VIOLATION_FIELD_MISSING`, `VIOLATION_KIND_UNKNOWN`, `ONTOLOGY_REVISION_STALE`. Codes register in `crates/medulla-capabilities/src/error_routing.rs`. Writes: none. Depends on: R005, R006.1, MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R034. | Test: `tests/query/test_validate_shape_served.py::test_violation_matrix_five_kinds` (L4). Each kind has a violating fixture and a repaired control. It asserts the typed diagnostic with pinned revisions and evidence refs. A control with only `(a,p,b)` yields no asymmetry diagnostic. Graph state is unchanged afterwards. |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R078.7` | **Governed company-context logical view.** Stage: query planning, served `Uql` `FROM company_context`. Caller: served `Uql` → `dispatch_query_family` (`src/server/handlers/query/dispatch.rs:66`) → `handle_uql` (`src/server/handlers/query/uql_statement.rs:17`) → `company_context_view` (new, `crates/medulla-plan/src/views/company_context.rs`) under `inject_scope` over one pinned snapshot. It composes organization and role vocabulary (`crates/medulla-core/ontology/core-foundation-v1.ttl`, `company-v1.ttl`), registered data-source mappings and native time-series facts. Mapping: organizations to `:Organization`, roles to `:Role`, data assets to `:DataAsset` (`dataplatform-v1.ttl`), permissions to `:SecurityClearance`. Row type `CompanyContextRow{category, entity_id, source_id, source_revision, clearance}`. Time-series join key `(series_id, timestamp)` at the pinned snapshot. The view returns references and copies no source record. Refusals: `COMPANY_MAPPING_MISSING`, `COMPANY_MAPPING_AMBIGUOUS`, `PLAN_SCOPE_UNVERIFIED`, `SOURCE_REVISION_STALE`, `CROSS_TENANT_JOIN`. No new ontology terms. Codes register in `crates/medulla-capabilities/src/error_routing.rs`. Writes: none. Depends on: R078.2, MEDULLA-DURABLE-KERNEL-R043, MEDULLA-CQ-XSRC, MEDULLA-UNIFIED-DATA-PLANE-R003. | Test: `tests/query/test_company_context_served.py::test_two_tenants_two_clearances` (L4). It asserts per-category source-bound references, denied rows excluded before any source read, and each refusal. The cross-source question is answered from ingested facts, not a hand-written graph. |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R081.9.7.7` | **RETIRED: merged into the traversal behavior row.** The zero-model-call rule and the three granularities now live in the behavior row `.2` and the model row `.1`. The mode adds no second retriever. | Retired; see `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R081.9.7.2`. |

| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R040.2.7` | **Topic fusion: per-document topic responsibilities.** Stage: query execution. Caller: served `UnifiedQuery` → `dispatch_query_family` (`src/server/handlers/query/dispatch.rs:66`) → `run_unified_with` (`src/server/handlers/query/planning.rs:436`) → `medulla_plan::execute` (`crates/medulla-plan/src/exec.rs:536`) → `fuse_topic_dense` → `TopicBackend::assignments`. Decision: assignments are backend-computed at query time and are not stored, so no ingest step writes them. `DocumentTopicAssignment{document_id, content_revision, corpus_generation, backend_id, backend_revision, space_tuple, topic_id, responsibility}` is defined in `crates/medulla-plan/src/exec/topic.rs`. The planner computes each candidate's topic component from its revision-matched assignments and feeds it to R040.2.2. Hard constraints, `w`, tie order and tenant scope stay unchanged. Refusals: `TOPIC_ASSIGNMENT_MISSING`, `TOPIC_ASSIGNMENT_DUPLICATE`, `TOPIC_ASSIGNMENT_STALE`, `TOPIC_SPACE_MISMATCH`. None falls back to dense-only ranking. <!-- spec-definition-patch:reviewed-mapped-subsets-20261012 -->
## Reviewed mapped definitions — 2026-10-12

These append-only children preserve the existing parent contracts named below. All remain SPECIFIED. This definition records planned acceptance and does not claim implementation or served receipts.

| ID | Requirement | Verification |
|---|---|---|
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R081.8.100` | **Retriever-bound query strategy and transfer revalidation.** Subsystem: retrieval decisions, feedback step. Caller: `run_round` → `apply_feedback` → `check_strategy_binding`. Add `QueryStrategy { strategy_id, digest, retriever_id, retriever_version, corpus_revision, model_revision, policy_revision, evaluation_set_digest }` and `TransferReceipt { target_retriever_id, target_retriever_version, strategy_id, strategy_digest, evaluation_set_digest, metric_id, measured, threshold, passed, corpus_revision, model_revision, policy_revision }` in `crates/medulla-types/src/retrieval_round.rs`. Add `check_strategy_binding` in `crates/medulla-plan/src/retrieval_feedback.rs`. A strategy is reusable without a receipt only on an exact match. Retriever id, version, corpus, model and policy revisions must be byte-identical. A changed target needs a `TransferReceipt` with `passed` true, `measured >= threshold` and the same digests. A receipt below threshold refuses activation and keeps the prior strategy. No strategy is copied to another retriever. Refusal: `RETRIEVAL_STRATEGY_UNVERIFIED`. The `threshold` is a request value that must be at least `knob retrieval.transfer_min_threshold` (0.6, [0, 1], metric score). Depends on: R081.8.2. | Test: `crates/medulla-plan/src/retrieval_feedback.rs::tests::strategy_reuse_needs_matching_retriever_or_receipt` (L1) and `tests/retrieval_round_served.rs::transfer_receipt_gates_strategy_reuse` (L3). It asserts same-retriever reuse, a version change refusal, a below-threshold refusal and an unchanged active strategy. |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R053.100` | **Generated `Cypher` property keys use backtick quoting with a build gate.** Stage: federation planning. Caller: served `Uql` `FOREIGN` over a `Cypher` source → `federation_cypher_unbound.rs` renderer → `quote_property_key` (new, same file). Every generated property key is a backtick-quoted identifier; an embedded backtick doubles. Values stay parameters. One renderer serves every `Cypher` path; it never reuses SQL quoting. Build gate: `scripts/check_cypher_render.sh` (new) runs in the lint step of `.github/workflows/ci.yml`. It parses generated fixtures with the `Cypher` grammar. It fails on any unquoted or malformed key. Refusal at runtime: `CYPHER_KEY_INVALID`. Codes register in `crates/medulla-capabilities/src/error_routing.rs`. Writes: none; the query path is read-only. Depends on: R053, MEDULLA-DURABLE-KERNEL-R024.3, R069. | Test: `crates/medulla-plan/src/federation_cypher_unbound.rs::tests::key_quoting_fixtures` (L2). Fixtures: plain, reserved, spaced, Unicode, embedded backtick and one unescaped key that the gate rejects. Served proof: `tests/query/test_foreign_kinds_served.py::test_cypher_keys_quoted` (L4). |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R032.100` | **Layer- and history-weighted impact in both dependency directions.** Stage: query execution, served `Uql` `PROPAGATE ... RISK HISTORY`. Caller: served `Uql` → `dispatch_query_family` (`src/server/handlers/query/dispatch.rs:66`) → `handle_uql` (`src/server/handlers/query/uql_statement.rs:17`) → `propagate.rs::historical_risk` (new, `crates/medulla-plan/src/exec/propagate_history.rs`). Result type `HistoricalRisk` lives in `crates/medulla-types/src/impact_risk.rs`. It binds target node and revision, direction and bounded `HOPS`. It binds ordered layer weights and the failure dataset (id, revision, window, digest). It binds observation counts, policy revision and request digest. Rule: `failure_rate = failures / observations`. `layer_contribution = layer_weight * failure_rate`, both in `[0, 1]`. `path_risk = 1 - product(1 - layer_contribution)` over unique nodes of a path. Duplicate paths to a node take the maximum. Upstream and downstream path sets are listed separately. Repeated nodes inside one path are suppressed; ties order by hop count, node id, then edge ids. A node without history is `unknown` and the aggregate is `null` with a partial flag. The failure dataset is the outcome records of `MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE` written by `DecisionLog` (R801). Refusals: `HISTORY_DATASET_UNAVAILABLE`, `HISTORY_DATASET_UNAUTHORIZED`, `HISTORY_MALFORMED`. The score is an extra dimension and leaves `R032` results unchanged. Consumer: `MERISTEM-HARNESS-CYCLE-R065`. Writes: none. Depends on: R032, MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R801. | Test: `tests/query/test_propagate_served.py::test_historical_risk_both_directions` (L4). Fixtures cover three layers, differing failure counts, a cycle and missing history. It asserts both directions, the formulas, each refusal, and unchanged `R032` output. |
<!-- /spec-definition-patch -->

All rows are SPECIFIED contracts only. No implementation, test execution or served receipt is claimed. Existing parent and adjacent-owner obligations remain in force.

| ID | Requirement | Verification |
|---|---|---|
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R086` | **Inductive n-ary hypothesis-only link prediction (rollup).** Subsystem: graph learning. Entry point: new read-only `Method::GraphLearnPredictNary`. It returns unasserted hypotheses for ordered n-ary tuples. It does not mutate graph facts or replace the binary `GraphLearnPredict` contracts. Child `.100` and its children `.100.1` to `.100.4` deliver it. | Root L3 journey test: `tests/graphlearn_nary_served.rs::nary_hypotheses_are_read_only_and_ordered` (L3). |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R086.100` | **Ordered n-ary inductive link prediction emits hypotheses only (rollup).** Subsystem: graph learning. Entry point: new read-only `Method::GraphLearnPredictNary` with capability action `graph.learn.predict_nary`, which grants no write. It scores ordered n-ary tuples, including entities unseen at fit time, and returns hypotheses only. The binary `GraphLearnPredict` and its write authority stay unchanged. It is routed in `src/server/handlers/graphlearn.rs` as a read, not through the mutation gateway in `gateway_mining_ml.rs`. Caller chain: the `GraphLearnPredictNary` arm in `src/server/handlers/graphlearn.rs` → the new n-ary scorer in `crates/medulla-compute/src/graphlearn/nary.rs`. An empty ordered tuple is refused. A caller-asserted evidence class other than hypothesis is refused. The reply carries no assertion flag. Children `.1` to `.4` deliver it. Consumer: MERISTEM-HARNESS-CYCLE-R066. | Root L3 journey test: `tests/graphlearn_nary_served.rs::nary_hypotheses_are_read_only_and_ordered` (L3). It also asserts the empty-tuple refusal, the evidence-class refusal and the absent assertion flag. |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R081.101` | **Reusable compressed document state with decoding-free reranking (rollup).** Subsystem: retrieval decisions, selection step. Entry point: served `Method::RetrievalRound`. The engine builds a query-independent document state once and scores queries against stored projections with no decoding or generation. Children `.1` to `.3` deliver it. The encoder is a server-bound `StateEncoder` port, as the reranker is in R081.7.3. | Root L3 journey test: `tests/retrieval_round_served.rs::compressed_state_rerank_is_order_independent` (L3). |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R081.102` | **Index-time distilled scorer with measured quality and zero query model calls.** Subsystem: retrieval decisions, selection step. Learn path: `DecisionFit` submits a `RerankHead` fitted offline by the harness from teacher judgments. The head follows the one head lifecycle (draft, evaluated, published, champion, retired) of MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R805. Medulla stores the artifact with corpus revision, index revision, teacher model revision, judgment dataset digest, artifact digest and metric policy. Medulla evaluates it on a held-out target set. It becomes champion only when the measured metric reaches `knob retrieval.distill_min_quality` (0.8, [0, 1], nDCG at 10). Query caller: `run_round` → `score_with_head` applies the champion as a linear head over stored features. It makes no model-service call and never distills on demand. A below-threshold, stale or unbound artifact refuses with `RETRIEVAL_POLICY_UNVERIFIED` and the prior champion stays active. Depends on: MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R805, MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R179. | Test: `tests/retrieval_round_served.rs::distilled_head_activates_only_above_threshold` (L3) fits, evaluates and promotes a head. Queries make zero model-service calls. After a below-threshold artifact the prior champion stays. |

<!-- spec-definition-patch:final-mapping-eg41-20261012 -->
## Additional reviewed mapped definition — 2026-10-12

| ID | Requirement | Verification |
|---|---|---|
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R081.100` | **Joint sparse nonnegative reconstruction selects a diverse document set.** Subsystem: retrieval decisions, selection step. Caller: `Method::RetrievalRound` → `run_round` → `joint_sparse_select` in new `crates/medulla-numeric/src/sparse_select.rs`. The `selection` part gains `mode: JointSparse { lambda, tolerance, max_iters, embedding_revision }`. Given query vector q and candidate matrix V, the kernel minimizes `||q - Vᵀx||₂² + λ||x||₁` with x ≥ 0 by cyclic coordinate descent. The update is `x_i = max(0, (v_i·r_i - λ/2) / ||v_i||²)`, where `r_i` is the residual without term `i`. It stops when the largest coefficient change is below `tolerance`. Knobs: `knob retrieval.joint_lambda` 0.1 [0.0001, 10] unitless. `knob retrieval.joint_tolerance` 0.000001 [0.000000001, 0.001] unitless. `knob retrieval.joint_max_iters` 1000 [10, 100000] sweeps. Rows of V are embeddings in one declared space and revision. The result lists document ids, coefficients above 0, residual and solver status, in coefficient-descending then id order. It never replaces the dual-score mode of R081.7. Tenant and result-count bounds apply. Refusals: `RETRIEVAL_EMBEDDING_REVISION_MISMATCH`, `RETRIEVAL_POLICY_UNVERIFIED` for invalid lambda, `RETRIEVAL_SOLVER_NOT_CONVERGED`. Depends on: R081.7.3. | Test: `crates/medulla-numeric/src/sparse_select.rs::tests::matches_exhaustive_oracle_on_tiny_fixture` (L2) and `tests/retrieval_round_served.rs::joint_selection_prefers_diverse_pair` (L3). The served test uses two correlated documents and a diverse pair, and asserts exact selection and order. |
<!-- /spec-definition-patch -->

Every parsed planner candidate is grounded against caller-authorized schemas before execution. It checks each named label, property, relationship and foreign source. It repairs only proven stored-name aliases, returns typed feedback for unknown identifiers and refuses exhausted correction steps without executing. Child `.1` (graph snapshot) is landed. Child `.2` (source-native identifiers) has its own children `.2.1` to `.2.5`. | `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R058.4.7.1` | **Snapshot graph and authorized foreign-source grounding.** `SchemaContext` in `crates/medulla-plan/src/nl/context.rs` reads the caller's complete row-filtered snapshot. No fixed node-scan bound can classify an unobserved name as absent. It holds each label with its stored property keys, each edge relationship and any declared foreign sources. The schema hint lists them. The served path obtains foreign names from the existing owner-authorized source registry before planning. An absent registry refuses foreign stages with `NL_SCHEMA_UNBOUND_FOREIGN_SOURCE`. `plan_grounded` parses every candidate with the caller grammar. It then checks each label, predicate property, relationship and `FOREIGN` source against the schema, including operations nested in fusion branches. A local `MATCH` scan always uses local labels, even before or after a foreign-source stage. Foreign operators carry ids and scores, while property reads rematerialize the same local snapshot. Foreign replacement, JOIN and mixed fusion cannot exempt an unknown local property. A JOIN can reseed foreign ids when its input is empty, so its output uses the complete local property schema. Inline adapters need the separate authority and schema work in `.9`. Label-bearing keys remain queryable properties, and unlabelled node fields are included for `MATCH ()`. Traversal and propagation predicates use stored edge keys, not node keys. Attribution input properties and Owen grouping properties use the incoming node scope. SPARQL, inferred-class, spatial and tensor source stages reset the incoming node scope. Spatial and tensor layer names use the graph labels their executors read. JSONPath checks exact top-level document keys; nested document shape and wildcard paths retain executor semantics and are not inferred or repaired. The engine rewrites a name that differs from a stored name only in case or separators. The disclosed UQL is then the canonical text of the plan that runs. Any other unknown name is a typed violation. The codes are `NL_SCHEMA_UNKNOWN_LABEL`, `NL_SCHEMA_UNKNOWN_PROPERTY`, `NL_SCHEMA_UNKNOWN_RELATIONSHIP`, `NL_SCHEMA_UNKNOWN_SOURCE` and `NL_SCHEMA_UNBOUND_FOREIGN_SOURCE`. The engine returns each parse error or violation to the planner with its reason. After `max_steps` (default 2), `NlQuery` and `NlQueryExplain` refuse with `INVALID_ARGUMENT`. The refusal carries the text and outcome of every step, and nothing runs. The system prompt states that example names are not the schema. | A table holds the 26 validation-run questions over ingested ServiceNow fixture data. Each row asserts grounded UQL and the expected rows. A question with no graph data asserts the typed refusal (`context_tests.rs::evidence_questions_are_grounded_or_refused_over_servicenow_data`). A served test refuses an unknown property with its reason and runs a repaired name (`nl_explain.rs::nl_query_grounds_names_on_the_graph_schema_before_running`). |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R058.4.7.2` | **Source-native identifier grounding (rollup).** Stage: query planning. Caller: served `NlQuery` → `dispatch_query_family` → `handle_nl_query_plan` (`src/server/handlers/query/nl.rs:58`) → `ground_candidate`. Grounding covers five source families, one child each. `.2.1` covers decision-record columns and predicates. `.2.2` covers time-series names and channels. `.2.3` covers CEP attribute names. `.2.4` covers UDF identifiers. `.2.5` covers embedded RDF and `SPARQL` identifiers. Each child names the existing validator it reuses and returns schema feedback before execution. Depends on: R058.4.7. | The child acceptance criteria apply. Root journey test: `tests/nl_query_grounding.rs::source_native_identifiers_grounded` (L3). |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R058.4.8` | **The hint offers mapped connector properties under their ontology names.** The served handler reads the composed GraphSchema properties through the `OntologyInspect` vocabulary. It links each property to one stored key of a label in its `rdfs:domain`. A property without a domain links to any label. The key must normalize to the property's local name or its `rdfs:label`. So the hint lists ServiceNow `assigned_to` as `assignedTo = assigned_to`. A predicate on the ontology name runs on the stored key. An ambiguous or out-of-domain property links nothing. | Tests link the ServiceNow pack properties onto ingested records and show them in the hint (`context_tests.rs`). They run an ontology-named predicate on the stored key and check the domain and ambiguity rules. |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R058.4.9` | **Inline foreign adapter grounding.** Stage: query planning. Caller: served `NlQuery` → `dispatch_query_family` → `handle_nl_query_plan` (`src/server/handlers/query/nl.rs:58`) → `ground_candidate`. Decision: natural-language grounding always refuses an inline `ForeignScan` adapter with `NL_SCHEMA_UNBOUND_FOREIGN_SOURCE`. Only registered named sources ground, because a registered source carries a declared schema and an owner. Direct deterministic `Uql` and `UnifiedQuery` keep their existing adapter policy. Registered named-source behavior is unchanged. Codes register in `crates/medulla-capabilities/src/error_routing.rs`. Writes: none. Depends on: R058.4.7, R073. | Test: `tests/nl_query_grounding.rs::inline_adapter_refused_named_source_grounds` (L3). It asserts the refusal for an inline HTTP adapter and success for a registered source. |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R058.5` | **Configurable NL planner request.** The standalone planner reads a read timeout and an extra request body. They come from the same configuration as the endpoint and model. The `nl_query` or `llm` config section holds `read_timeout_secs` (default 30, accepted 1 to 600). It also holds `extra_body`, a JSON object that the planner merges into the request top level. The default `extra_body` is `{"chat_template_kwargs":{"enable_thinking":false}}`, so a reasoning model answers directly. The env keys `MEDULLA_NL_READ_TIMEOUT_SECS` and `MEDULLA_NL_EXTRA_BODY` (JSON) override the file. A configured `extra_body` replaces the default and cannot set `model` or `messages`. The planner refuses invalid values with the typed `NlConfigError` (`ReadTimeoutOutOfRange`, `ExtraBodyNotObject`, `ExtraBodyReservedKey`, `InvalidValue`). `Method::NlQuery` then refuses with the `ENGINE_UNAVAILABLE` code and a detail that names the bad key. | Tests against a local stub HTTP server confirm the default and a configured extra body reach the request body. They also confirm a peer slower than the configured read timeout fails within bounds. Refusal tests confirm each `NlConfigError` variant. A config test confirms the keys parse and invalid values are refused. An unparseable env or config value is refused with `InvalidValue`, which carries the source name and the raw text. A handler test confirms the served refusal carries the code and names the key. |

Co-cover risk: one evidence node is adjacent to at least 2 clue nodes outside the chain. Exposed constant: a clue text shares at least `knob search_task.expose_overlap` (default 0.5, [0.1, 1.0], ratio) of the answer-name tokens. Prior-knowledge risk: the answer node degree is at least `knob search_task.prior_degree` (default 50, [5, 10,000], edges). Refinement adds one clue or masks one constant, for at most `knob search_task.refine_rounds` (default 3, [0, 8], rounds). Each detector is linear in the subgraph. A `Spark` submission id is the SHA-256 digest (64 lowercase hex characters) of preset id, input digests and parameters. Resubmission returns the same id and runs once. Cost: `O(input bytes)` hashing and 8 MiB memory. Codes register in `crates/medulla-capabilities/src/error_routing.rs`. Writes: the submission record through the jobs path. It runs through `Method::Sparql` with the spatial index. Create the `benches` directory. Budget: p99 at most 40 ms. A regression above 10 percent fails the nightly run and never gates a PR. | `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R720` | **One UQL plan across graph, vector, SQL, SPARQL and epistemic stages (rollup).** Subsystem: query and reasoning. Entry: served `Method::Uql` → `handle_uql`. One UQL program must compile to one plan DAG with one root. The DAG may mix graph traversal, vector similarity, SQL analytics, SPARQL with OWL entailment and epistemic stages. Every stage exchanges the `RowSet` of `crates/medulla-plan/src/rowset.rs`. Consumer: `MEDULLA-CQ-XSRC-R009` uses `DECAY` and `CLUSTER BY EMBEDDING` in one served program. Children `.1` to `.6` deliver it. Order: `.1` before `.2` and `.3`. | Root test `tests/uql_single_plan_all_modalities.rs::five_stage_kinds_one_plan` (L3) passes through every child. |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R720.1` | **Epistemic row fields.** `Row` (`crates/medulla-plan/src/rowset.rs`, struct with `id` and `score`) gains `confidence: Option<f32>`, `valid_time_ms: Option<i64>` and `tx_time_ms: Option<i64>`. `RowSet::from_rows` keeps its tuple signature and sets the new fields to `None`. A new `RowSet::from_epistemic_rows` takes the full row. A stage that reads none of the fields passes them through unchanged. Caller: `execute` builds rows in the traversal and vector stages. Depends on: none. | Test: `crates/medulla-plan/src/rowset.rs::tests::epistemic_fields_survive_traversal_and_vector` (L2) round-trips the three fields. |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R720.2` | **Confidence, belief and decay stages.** Caller: `handle_uql` → `execute` → `crates/medulla-plan/src/uql/grammar.rs` productions. `CONFIDENCE` (`confidence`) drops rows below its threshold. `BELIEF AS OF` (`belief_as_of`) reads the bitemporal state at its time. Both exist and bind here. New stage `DECAY HALF_LIFE <seconds>` multiplies each row score by `0.5^(age / half_life)`, where age is now minus `valid_time_ms` in seconds, as in `crates/medulla-numeric/src/risk/reputation.rs`. A row with no score takes score 1.0 before the decay. Depends on: MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R720.1. | Test: `crates/medulla-plan/tests/uql_epistemic_stages.rs::confidence_asof_decay` (L2) checks each stage on a fixed fixture. Verified by the root test. |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R720.3` | **Embedding-similarity clustering stage.** Caller: `execute` → new `Op::ClusterByEmbedding { k }` → `medulla_compute::mining::cluster::cluster` (`crates/medulla-compute/src/mining/cluster.rs`). Grammar: `CLUSTER BY EMBEDDING K <n>`. Distance is cosine. The seed is fixed at 0 so results repeat. Each row gains `cluster_id` and `centroid_distance` value channels. Refusal codes: see R720.5. Depends on: MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R720.1. | Test: `crates/medulla-plan/tests/uql_epistemic_stages.rs::cluster_by_embedding_is_deterministic` (L2) checks fixed clusters with seed 0. |

The served `UqlResult` reports the count of distinct plan ids. Depends on: ARBORA-OPS-R018.2.1. | Test: `tests/uql_single_plan_all_modalities.rs::program_runs_under_one_plan_id` (L3) asserts the plan id count equals 1 in EXPLAIN. |

Add both codes to `ServerErrorCode`. A refused program returns no partial rows. | `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R722` | **Connector data resources register as virtual-graph sources at pack import (rollup).** Subsystem: query and reasoning with sources. Entry: served `ConnectorPack.Import`, then served `Method::Uql`. The pack contract of MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R101 and SCION-CONNECTOR-CONTROL-R074 declares each data resource with its class, access method and pagination. Import registers one `vg:SourceConnection`, one `vg:VirtualMapping` and one typed foreign source per MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R073. A mapping serves reads only after admission approves it (ARBORA-FLEET-R042). Queries resolve through the active contract version of MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R107. Each read records usage and freshness on the catalog asset of MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R106. The engine reads at query time and copies nothing, except under a materialisation policy of MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.6. `SCION-CONNECTOR-CONTROL-R069.3` is the provider that refuses a descriptor whose access kind has no registered transport. Children `.2` to `.10` deliver it. | Root test `tests/federated/test_pack_source_served.py::test_uql_joins_native_fact_to_live_connector_rows_with_zero_copy` (L4). |

A signed import test checks held claims, tenant isolation and store reopen. |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R722.2` | **Registration and digest behavior.** Caller: `ConnectorPack.Import` → `src/server/handlers/admin/connector_pack/import.rs` → outbox → `src/server/connector_pack_projection.rs::project`. Import registers each declared resource as a held source and mapping. An `approved` mapping serves reads and a `held` one serves none. A re-import with an unchanged contract digest changes nothing. A changed contract replaces the mapping and returns it to `held`. The digest is the `body_sha256` of the authoritative current member body, never caller text. An imported publisher cannot set `approved`. Depends on: MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R101, MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R107. | Test: `crates/medulla-plan/tests/pack_source.rs::held_serves_none_approved_serves_rows_and_changed_contract_returns_to_held` (L2). Verified by the root test. |

It supplies no authority and registers nothing for an owner. The admitted projection hands a verified owner-context handoff to `ForeignSourceCatalog::register` (`src/server/foreign_catalog.rs`). Reads go through `foreign_scan` (`crates/medulla-plan/src/federation_opt/run.rs`). `ForeignSourceRegistry` keeps the registered spec identity and fingerprint. The verified bound driver of R722.9 takes precedence in naive and optimized resolution. It returns `Oq2Unbound` when no valid binding exists. Depends on: MEDULLA-UNIFIED-DATA-PLANE-R002.1, MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R722.2. | Test: `tests/federated/test_pack_source_served.py::test_import_registers_through_the_one_catalog` (L4). |

Import refuses a contract that names a literal secret with `CONTRACT_SECRET` (exists in `crates/medulla-rdf/src/pack/sources.rs`). The outbound guard of MEDULLA-UNIFIED-DATA-PLANE-R002.1 refuses a forbidden destination. A query over a `held` mapping returns `SOURCE_HELD` and names the mapping. A source that is down returns `SOURCE_UNAVAILABLE`, answers the native rows and flags the gap. Add the two new codes to `ServerErrorCode`. Depends on: MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R722.3. | Test: `tests/federated/test_pack_source_served.py::test_secret_guard_held_and_unavailable_refuse_with_named_codes` (L4). |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R722.5` | **Evidence.** Caller: `foreign_scan`. Each answer row from a pack source carries the connector, the contract IRI, the mapping IRI and the fetch time. The read increments the usage counter and sets the freshness age on the catalog asset of MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R106. A store audit shows zero source rows written for a non-materialised source. Depends on: MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R106. | Test: `tests/federated/test_pack_source_served.py::test_rows_carry_contract_provenance_and_nothing_is_copied` (L4). |

The test approves the mapping through `ApproveSource`. It runs a UQL query that joins a native fact to the connector rows. It then counts source rows in the store. The `FixtureSource` of `tests/connector_competency` (`MEDULLA-CQ-CONN-R067.8`) consumes this fixture connector. | `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R040.5.1` | **Low-storage ANN: manifest and byte accounting.** Stage: ingest, index build. Caller: ingest worker loop → `dispatch` (`src/server/handlers/semantic_index/dispatch.rs:14`) → ANN build → `LowStorageAnnManifest::admit` (new, `crates/medulla-ann/src/low_storage.rs`). Types `LowStorageAnnManifest` and `StorageAccounting` sit beside HNSW and IVF-PQ. The manifest binds tenant, source snapshot, source id, revision, text digest, embedding space, encoding policy, generation, build policy and limits. Accounting sums graph, code, metadata, source-reference, cache and scratch bytes with checked addition. The artifact references source text and never copies it. Knob: config `ann_storage_ceiling_bytes` in `SchedulerConfig` (`crates/medulla-types/src/scheduler_config.rs`), default 2147483648, `[1048576, 1099511627776]`. Refusals: `ANN_MANIFEST_INVALID` (missing identity, mixed policies, non-finite counts), `ANN_BYTES_OVERFLOW`, `ANN_OVER_CEILING`, all before publication. Codes register in `crates/medulla-capabilities/src/error_routing.rs`. Writes: the manifest with the generation. Depends on: none. | Test: `crates/medulla-ann/src/low_storage.rs::tests::manifest_round_trip_and_sum` (L1). It checks every field and an independent byte sum. Verified by the root slice test `tests/retrieval/test_low_storage_ann_served.py::test_selectivity_bound_and_readback`. |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R040.5.2` | **Low-storage ANN: compact graph artifact.** Stage: ingest, index build. Caller: `LowStorageAnnManifest::admit` → `HnswIndex::to_compact` (new, `crates/medulla-ann`). Reuse `HnswIndex` traversal, the artifact codec and `IvfPq` SQ8 persistence. The compact mode stores stable row ids, bounded adjacency (at most `M` neighbours per node), compressed codes and tombstones. Format version byte `2`; existing version `1` artifacts stay readable. It adds no second authority and no second store. Refusal: `ANN_ARTIFACT_INVALID` for truncation, duplicates, out-of-range edges, shape mismatch, unknown version or missing source generation. Codes register in `crates/medulla-capabilities/src/error_routing.rs`. Writes: the artifact under the index directory. Depends on: R040.5.1. | Test: `crates/medulla-ann/src/low_storage.rs::tests::compact_decode_and_refusals` (L1). It decodes adjacency, codes, tombstones and ids and compares candidates with the full-vector oracle. Verified by the root slice test. |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R040.5.3` | **Low-storage ANN: revision-bound regeneration and rescore.** Stage: ingest and query. Caller: ingest worker loop → `dispatch` → `SemanticStore::regenerate_eligible` (`crates/medulla-core/src/compute/semantic_regeneration.rs:208`). Decision: `regenerate_eligible` is the single write path; `MEDULLA-REPO-INGEST-R006.2` calls it. A replacement publishes only after `SemanticStore`, `GenerationStamp`, fences and `EmbeddingVersionStore` verify a complete generation. Compact search returns candidates without source fetches. Exact rescore fetches authorized rows under three budgets. Keys: `rescore_max_items` (default 200, `[1, 10000]`), `rescore_max_bytes` (default 8388608, `[65536, 1073741824]`), `rescore_max_ms` (default 500, `[10, 60000]`). Config in `SchedulerConfig` (`crates/medulla-types/src/scheduler_config.rs`). Results bind source digest, space, policy and generation. Refusals: `ANN_SOURCE_STALE`, `RESCORE_BUDGET_EXHAUSTED`. Codes register in `crates/medulla-capabilities/src/error_routing.rs`. Writes: the replacement generation. Depends on: R040.5.2, MEDULLA-REPO-INGEST-R006.2. | Test: `tests/retrieval/test_low_storage_ann_served.py::test_selective_regeneration_and_rescore` (L4). It changes one source revision and asserts selective regeneration. It asserts no source fetch on compact search. A stale identity keeps the prior generation. |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R040.5.4` | **Low-storage ANN: bounded embedding provider and cache.** Stage: ingest. Caller: `regenerate_eligible` → `EmbeddingProvider` (`crates/medulla-core/src/compute/semantic.rs:302`, `BoundedCachingEmbeddingProvider` in `semantic_embedding_batch.rs:75`). The provider batches typed references under finite budgets. Config keys in `SchedulerConfig` (`crates/medulla-types/src/scheduler_config.rs`): `embed_batch_items` default 64 `[1, 1024]`. `embed_batch_bytes` default 1048576 `[4096, 67108864]`. `embed_concurrency` default 4 `[1, 64]`. `embed_calls_max` default 1000 per run `[1, 1000000]`. `embed_cache_entries` default 10000 `[0, 10000000]`. `embed_cache_bytes` default 67108864 `[0, 4294967296]`. The cache key is `(tenant, source_identity, digest, embedding_space, policy_revision)`. The cache is a recomputable accelerator, not a store, and does not reuse `medulla-kvcache`. `medulla-core` stays independent of `medulla-plan`. Query text keeps using `TextEmbedder`. Refusals: `EMBED_BUDGET_EXHAUSTED`, `EMBED_VECTOR_INVALID` (wrong width or non-finite), `EMBED_PROVIDER_FAILED`. Codes register in `crates/medulla-capabilities/src/error_routing.rs`. Writes: none beyond the generation. Depends on: R040.5.3. | Test: `crates/medulla-core/src/compute/semantic_embedding_batch.rs::tests::budgets_keys_and_duplicates` (L2). A hit never crosses tenant, digest, space or policy; duplicates count one effect. Verified by the root slice test. |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R040.5.5` | **Low-storage ANN: optional hub-preserving pruning.** Stage: ingest, index build. Caller: `HnswIndex::to_compact` → `prune_hubs` (new, `crates/medulla-ann/src/low_storage.rs`). `HubPruningPolicy{degree_threshold, retention, tie_rule, revision, seed_digest}`; config key `ann_hub_pruning` in `SchedulerConfig` (`crates/medulla-types/src/scheduler_config.rs`), default `disabled`. The default fixture policy: `degree_threshold` 32, `retention` 0.5, `tie_rule` node id ascending. Pruning removes redundant non-hub edges only and keeps hubs. The manifest records the policy; a caller cannot infer it from untrusted text. Disabled pruning leaves topology byte-identical. Refusals: `ANN_PRUNING_INVALID` (bad bounds, missing digest, mismatched policy), `ANN_PRUNING_DISCONNECTED`. Codes register in `crates/medulla-capabilities/src/error_routing.rs`. Writes: the pruned artifact. Depends on: R040.5.2. | Test: `crates/medulla-ann/src/low_storage.rs::tests::pruning_disabled_identical_enabled_connected` (L1). Each fixture reports recall and storage with no universal target. Verified by the root slice test. |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R040.5.6` | **Low-storage ANN: authenticated integrated path and measured proof.** Stage: query execution. Caller: served `UnifiedQuery` → `dispatch_query_family` (`src/server/handlers/query/dispatch.rs:66`) → `run_unified_with` (`src/server/handlers/query/planning.rs:436`) → `medulla_plan::execute` (`crates/medulla-plan/src/exec.rs:536`) → `LowStorageAnnManifest` receipt fields via `PlanCtx`. The receipt binds source, model, space, build workspace, policy, generation, accounting, counters and result digests. It adds no served operation, MCP server, harness store or source registry. The proof compares the path with an exact full-scan baseline on fixed workloads. It records bytes, rows, visits, latencies, rescore, batch, cache and recall. No external storage figure is a target. Refusals: budgets exhausted, stale generations, unauthorized reads and incomplete receipts fail before any effect. Depends on: R040.5.1, R040.5.2, R040.5.3, R040.5.4. | Test: `tests/retrieval/test_low_storage_ann_served.py::test_selectivity_bound_and_readback` (L4). A temporary engine builds, updates, restarts, queries and reads back. It asserts the `4 * result_rows + 64` visit bound at the three corpus sizes and every refusal. |

Scatter cap is `min(pixel_width * pixel_height, 262144)` bins. Line cap is `min(4 * pixel_width, 65536)` bins. The response byte cap is `min(16777216, 65536 + 96 * max_bin_count)`, measured after serialization. The effective ceiling is `min(byte_ceiling, response cap)`. A ceiling too small for the envelope refuses `BYTE_CEILING_TOO_SMALL` before any scan. Scatter bins return stable id, count and axis extrema, using a uniform grid of the viewport domain. An asset flagged `deprecation` yields the typed warning `STORED_QUERY_DEPRECATED_ASSET` with the asset id. The save and the run still proceed. Writes: the warning list on the result record. Depends on: MEDULLA-TYPED-PACKS-R802.7. | Test: `tests/federated/test_stored_query_served.py::test_workspace_share_schedule_and_lint` (L4) deprecates a table and asserts the warning. |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R720.7` | **UQL stage REQUIRE NONEMPTY with a caller-named refusal code.** Subsystem: query. Entry: `Method::Uql`. Caller: `src/server/handlers/query/uql_statement.rs::handle_uql` → `crates/medulla-plan/src/uql/grammar.rs` stage `REQUIRE NONEMPTY '<CODE>'` → `crates/medulla-plan/src/exec.rs`. The stage refuses the whole program with `<CODE>` when its input `RowSet` has zero rows. A refused program returns no partial rows. `<CODE>` must match `^[A-Z][A-Z0-9_]{2,63}$`; else the parser refuses with `UQL_E_REQUIRE_CODE_INVALID`. Writes: none. Depends on: `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R720.4`. | Test: `crates/medulla-plan/tests/uql_epistemic_stages.rs::require_nonempty_refuses_with_code` (L2) asserts the code, the empty result and the invalid-code refusal. |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R722.7` | **Source approval compare-and-set.** Caller: `ConnectorPack` admin op `ConnectorPackOp::ApproveSource` (`crates/medulla-types/src/connector_pack/ops.rs`) → `src/server/handlers/admin/connector_pack/approval.rs`. The op admits only an exact current held registration. It performs the CAS on the current head and the body digest. A stale, absent or mismatched target refuses. Approval is a separate admitted operation, requested by arbora (ARBORA-FLEET-R042.1). Writes: the mapping state `approved`. Depends on: MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R722.2. | Test: `src/server/handlers/admin/connector_pack/tests/source_approval.rs::approve_source_requires_current_head_and_contract_digest` (L3). |

The planner pushes down only the pagination and key filters the contract declares. The exact local residual filter always stays, even when a source claims it filtered. Depends on: MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R722.3. | Test: `crates/medulla-plan/tests/pack_source.rs::undeclared_filters_stay_local_and_residual_always_runs` (L2). |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R722.9` | **Request-scoped driver binding.** Caller: arbora host binds a driver on an authenticated request → `src/server/foreign_read.rs`. EG re-reads the approved current registration. It matches the tuple `(connector, contract_iri, resource_uri, source_kind, class_iri, operation, entity, key_field, contract_digest, body_sha256)` and the immutable plan and query digests. Verified context supplies tenant, owner, actor, delegation and authority. The driver payload supplies none of them. Refusal: `FOREIGN_READ_PLAN_MISMATCH` or `FOREIGN_READ_QUERY_MISMATCH`, both in `ServerErrorCode`. Depends on: MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R722.3. | Test: `tests/test_uql_foreign_execution.py::test_driver_binding_rejects_payload_authority` (L4). |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R722.10` | **Foreign read lifecycle.** Caller: arbora `FleetSourceExecutor` (`arbora/fleet/source_executor.py`) calls, through `fleet.call` with read effect, `ForeignReadOpen`, `ForeignReadChunk`, `ForeignReadComplete` and `ForeignReadAbort`. Types: `crates/medulla-types/src/foreign_read.rs`. A server-owned opaque handle and a monotone sequence bind to tenant, owner, actor, plan and registration. Every chunk carries the server-derived identity `{source_ref, source_revision, schema_digest, snapshot_ref}`. `ForeignReadRow.score` is `Option<f32>`, never binary64. Limits: positive finite row, byte and deadline values. Exactly one terminal outcome. Handles expire and refuse replay. EG opens no URL. Refusals: `FOREIGN_READ_SEQUENCE`, `FOREIGN_READ_REPLAY`, `FOREIGN_READ_EXPIRED`, `FOREIGN_READ_SOURCE_IDENTITY_STALE`, `FOREIGN_READ_TERMINAL`. Depends on: MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R722.9, ARBORA-FLEET-R042. | Test: `tests/test_uql_foreign_execution.py::test_lifecycle_sequence_replay_expiry_and_terminal` (L4). |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R723` | **Serve embedding admission and re-embedding as methods.** Stage: served operations over the semantic index worker. Caller: Arbora `ingest.embedding.*` → `eg.<service>.EmbeddingAdmit` and `Reembed` → `src/server/handlers/semantic_index/worker.rs`. Code: new Methods in `crates/medulla-types/src/protocol/method/method_10.rs`. `EmbeddingAdmit { model_id, revision, probe_set_digest }` runs the promotion gate of `MEDULLA-UNIFIED-DATA-PLANE-R040`. `Reembed { source_id, model_revision }` queues one durable job. Rule: the same worker serves row-write hooks (R077.3) and this method. A repeated key returns the same job. Refusals: `EMBEDDING_MODEL_NOT_ADMITTED`, `REEMBED_SOURCE_UNKNOWN`. Writes: the job record. Depends on: R039.2. | Test: `tests/semantic_index/test_reembed_methods_served.py::test_admit_then_reembed_one_job` (L4) asserts the gate, one job per key and both refusal codes. |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R724` | **Authorization-segmented schema projections method.** Stage: query path, new read `Method::SchemaProjections`. Code: request type in `crates/medulla-types/src/protocol/method/method_03.rs`, handler `src/server/handlers/query/schema_projections.rs`. Rule: return one entry per authorization boundary of the verified tenant. Each entry holds `boundary`, `required_scopes`, `tables` and `mapping_ref`. The read uses the row-level-security filtered snapshot. The engine filters by the caller's grants and returns the covered set only. A boundary count is visible only to operator scope `audit:operator`. Refusals: `ACCESS_DENIED`. Writes: none. Depends on: MEDULLA-IDENTITY-R001. Consumer: ARBORA-OPS-R052.2. | Test: `tests/served_schema_projections.rs::projections_follow_caller_grants_and_hide_other_boundaries` (L3), with a cross-tenant negative case. |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R725` | **Viewport bins and selection resolve methods.** Stage: query path, new reads `Method::ViewportBins` and `Method::GraphSelectionResolve`. Code: variants in `crates/medulla-types/src/protocol/method/method_03.rs`, kernels in `crates/medulla-numeric/src/viewport.rs`, handler `src/server/handlers/query/viewport.rs`. Rule: scatter returns a count grid of `width_px` by `height_px` with per-cell extrema. Line returns min and max per pixel column with explicit gaps. The result names `formula_revision`, `source_revision` and `viewport_revision`, and obeys the request `max_bytes` and `max_bins`. Refusals: `STALE_REVISION`, `LIMIT_EXCEEDED`. Writes: none. Consumer: ARBORA-OPS-R064.2. | Test: `tests/served_viewport_bins.rs::bins_match_independent_reference_and_obey_limits` (L3), with a cross-tenant negative case. |

Rule: add the clause `EVALUATE kernel OF c [AGAINST c2] [key value ...]` producing `Op::Evaluate`, a report stage after `SKILL`. Kernel names and parameter keys are the ones of `MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R002.2.3.15`. Caller: `UnifiedQuery` → parser → planner. Refusal: an unknown kernel returns `UQL_E_EVALUATE_KERNEL` at parse time. Depends-on: `MEDULLA-FINANCE-PRIMITIVES-R002.2.3.15`. | `crates/medulla-query/tests/uql_evaluate_grammar.rs::parses_every_kernel_and_refuses_unknown` (L1) and the served test of R002.2.3.15 (L4). |

It calls the served read `AtomContext` for passage context and cites each `provenance_key`. Provider: MEDULLA-INGEST-WORKER-R003.3. | `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R728` | **Exhaustive OpKind and PredKind printer and parser round trip.** Subsystem: query. Entry point: served `UnifiedQuery` parse and print. Caller: `UnifiedQuery` handler → `crates/medulla-plan/src/uql/parser` → `crates/medulla-plan/src/uql/print.rs`. One macro generates `medulla_types::wire::OpKind` and `PredKind` with their complete variant lists. A walk prints, parses and canonicalizes every variant against an empty builder-only allowlist. A property test runs 512 cases of print, parse and compare. The fuzz target `crates/medulla-plan/fuzz/fuzz_targets/uql_parse.rs` asserts the same round trip. Refusal: a variant missing from the printer fails the build test with `UQL_VARIANT_UNPRINTABLE`. Knobs: none. Writes: none. Depends on: MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R061. | Test: `crates/medulla-plan/src/uql/tests/roundtrip.rs::every_op_and_pred_kind_round_trips` (L2) and `::roundtrip_property_512_cases` (L2). |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R729` | **Federated pushdown to connector raw-source tools.** Subsystem: federated query planner. Caller: the served UQL or search method → planner pushdown. For a source that declares `access_kind: raw`, the executor calls the connector tools through the arbora fleet path. The tools are `raw_search`, `raw_read_span` and `raw_check_context`. It passes `max_scan_bytes` derived from the request budget. It converts the returned `scan_bytes` to cost units with `knob federated.raw_scan_cost_per_kib` (default 1, range [1, 100], unit cost units per KiB). An `OVER_CEILING` answer becomes the typed over-budget refusal of this spec. Admission and the principal are those of the caller. Depends on: SCION-SOURCE-INGEST-R903.3. | Test: `tests/federated/test_raw_pushdown_served.py::test_raw_source_search_is_budgeted_and_refuses_over_ceiling` (L3). A loopback connector serves the tools; the test asserts the cost report and the refusal. |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R730` | **The unified planner earns its scope through a falsification gate.** Caller: the nightly suite (`MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R178`) runs `benches/unified_vs_contracts.rs`. Workload: 10,000 claims with 1,024-dimension embeddings and 8 attributes, under 4 concurrent writers. The query is a two-hop neighbourhood joined with the top 50 vector matches and an attribute filter. Arm A runs one R720 plan in one engine. Arm B runs the vector match and the graph query on two engine instances. The client joins their results over the public contract. Rule: arm A passes when its p95 latency is at most 0.7 times arm B's. Recall at 50 must be equal, and no write may be lost. A failed run records a `PLANNER_SCOPE_FROZEN` decision (`MEDULLA-SDLC-R005`). While that decision stands, no new R720 or R721 operator may land. The thresholds are `Fixed` knobs. | Test: `benches/unified_vs_contracts.rs` (L6), checked by `tests/test_unified_planner_gate.py::test_failed_gate_records_the_freeze_decision`. |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R731` | **Queries declare a latency class.** Caller: the served `UnifiedQuery` and SPARQL handlers. Every query carries the class `interactive` (the default) or `analytical`. An interactive query reads only materialized entailments and runs no live reasoning. It must answer within p99 200 ms at the reference load and within `knob query.interactive_node_budget` (default 100,000 nodes, range [1,000, 10,000,000]). Exceeding the budget refuses with `QUERY_BUDGET_EXCEEDED`, with a hint to resubmit as analytical. An analytical query runs as a durable job and stores its result. Entailment maintenance runs incrementally off the write path. | Test: `tests/query/test_latency_classes_served.py::test_interactive_budget_refuses_and_analytical_runs_as_job` (L4). |
| `MEDULLA-FEDERATED-QUERY-R732` | **The unified planner improves through the evolution loop.** Caller: the R720 plan executor records each executed plan through `DecisionLog`. The record holds the chosen plan, its estimated cost, its measured cost and the result size. The planner's cost-model coefficients and operator choices are knobs (`MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R179`), and a fitted cost head ranks candidate plans. A revised knob set or cost head is a challenger in the `MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R805` lifecycle. It shadows the champion on recorded queries and is scored by `MEDULLA-DECISION-ENGINE-R811` receipts. It is promoted only when its p95 estimate error and p95 latency both improve. The R730 gate reruns against each new champion. A challenger that loses on either measure stays a challenger and records its loss. | Test: `tests/query/test_planner_evolution_served.py::test_recorded_plans_train_a_challenger_that_promotes_only_on_improvement` (L4). |
